予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

2026年度

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公的固定資本形成は、前期比▲2.4％と予測する。 
公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として 輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。 
2026年 7-9月期の地域別輸出数量指数を季節調整
値（当研究所による試算値）でみると、米国向けが
前期比 0.9％（4-6月期：同 2.8％） 、EU向けが前期
比▲2.4％（4-6月期：同 0.3％） 、アジア向けが前期
比▲0.3％（4-6 月期：同▲0.8％） 、うち中国向けが

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）予測

～ほぼゼロ成長も、緩やかな景気回復は継続～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田 隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表する 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比＋0.0%（前期比年
率換算＋0.1%）と予測する。設備投資や政府消費の増加に支えられ、かろうじて 4 四半期連続のプラス成長を
維持したものの、個人消費や輸出が前期の一時的な押し上げ要因の剥落もあって減少したことで、ほぼゼロ成長
にとどまったと見込まれる。なお、輸入が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は大きめのマイナスと
なるものの、公的在庫のプラス寄与により、おおむね相殺されたと考えられる。 
また、名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.9%（年率換算＋3.8%）となったと見込まれる。コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進
む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比＋2.7%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.9%）と高めの伸びが続いた。 
今回、7～9 月期の実質 GDP はほぼゼロ成長にとどまる見込みだが、イラン情勢の悪化という逆風を踏まえれ
ば、国内景気は底堅さを維持している 。先行きについては、輸入コストの高止まりに伴う価格転嫁の進展が景気
の重しとなる可能性はあるものの、良好な雇用・所得環境や企業業績の改善を背景に内需の回復基調が維持さ
れることなどから、国内景気は緩やかな回復を続けると期待される。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度 
（出所）内閣府「四半期別 GDP 速報」により作成

### Page 2

2 / 2

需要項目別の動向は以下の通りである。

① 実質個人消費（前期比 －0.1%） 
 良好な所得環境が維持され る中、消費者マインドも持ち直しへ向かったものの、前期に 耐久財
が自動車やエアコンを中心に 一時的な要因によって増加した反動 が生じた とみられる 。価格転
嫁の進展による 物価上昇 、地震や台風といった自然災害の発生 も重しとなった 可能性がある 。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比 －0.3%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数 が横ばいとなる中 、建築コストが上昇していることもあり、 住
宅投資は小幅ながら減少が続いたとみられる 。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比 ＋0.9%） 
 企業の投資意欲は 省力化やデジタル需要 などに支えられて 堅調であり 、設備投資は 3 四半期
ぶりに増加 したと見込まれる 。機械設備投資の供給側統計である資本財総供給（除く輸送機械）
も 7～9 月期は前期比 ＋5.3%と増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度 －0.0%ポイント ） 
 前期に 供給制約懸念の高まりを受けて、メーカーが製品在庫などの積み増しを進めた 反動もあ
り、7～9 月期はマイナス 寄与になったと考えられる 。 
 なお、公的在庫 について は、政府による代替調達の進展により 石油備蓄の 減少ペースが大きく鈍
化したことから、前期比寄与度 ＋0.5% ポイントと プラス寄与になったと みられる 。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費支出（前期比＋ 0.3%） 
 高齢化が進む中、医療費 ・介護費を中心に 底堅く推移 したと予測する。  
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比 －2.7%） 
 建築・土木コストの上昇が続いている中、 7～8 月の建設総合統計（出来高ベース：公共） は弱
めの動きとなった ことから 、実質公共投資は 2 四半期 連続で 減少したと見込まれる。  
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度 －0.5%ポイント 、実質輸出：前期比 －0.5%、実質輸入：同 ＋1.7%） 
 輸出は 前期比 －0.5%と減少したと予測する。財は 堅調な 米国向け や中東向けの持ち直しを受
けて増加 した一方、サービスは 前期に 研究開発サービスが一時 的な 要因により上振れた 反動
で減少 したとみられる。  
 輸入 は前期比 ＋1.7%と増加したと 予測する。 特に財が鉱物性燃料 を中心に 持ち直した。ただ
し、サービス については 減少した 可能性がある 。 
 結果 、輸出から輸入を差し引いた 外需 の寄与度は －0.5%ポイント とマイナス になった と見込ま
れる。

Win Semi’s 3Q26 core business (operating income) was 49/41% 
higher than GSe/BBG consensus, mainly driven by a more favorable 
product mix with higher contribution from the high margin 
infrastructure business (accounted for 39% of total revenue in 3Q26 
vs. 34% in 2Q26), resulting in a 9.2ppt QoQ increase in 3Q26 GPM, 
and came in 6.9/6.1ppt above GSe/BBG consensus. Win Semi’s 
OPEX increased to NT$775mn (vs. NT$743mn in 2Q26). NI was 
67/110% higher than GSe/BBG consensus due to the high 
non-operating income of NT$698mn (account for 33% of total 
pretax income). 
For 4Q26, the company expects revenue to increase by low single 
digit QoQ, and expects cellular to see QoQ decline due to weak 
season while the remaining businesses (including 
infrastructure/Wi-Fi/optical) will see flattish to small QoQ increase. 
For GM, the company guides the 4Q26 level to maintain at 
high-thirties %, which is much better than both our prior 
expectation and BBG consensus at 31.8/31.4%. Management 
indicated 3Q26 UTR to be higher at 75% (was 65% in 2Q26), and 
expect to maintain at similar level in 4Q26.  
The company continues to highlight the progress with its AI-related 
business, with its new PD (photodiodes) products started to ramp 
up from 2H26, with more contribution expected in 2027 along with 
more PD capacity coming online. For the new LD (laser diodes) 
products, the company continues to work with multiple clients on 
multiple new solutions (including CW laser, EML solutions and 
VCSEL), but expects to see more contribution in 2028 due to the 
lengthy reliability tests (takes 6M+ per our estimates). The company 
reiterated that CAPEX this year is project driven, which will mainly 
focus on expanding InP and GaN capacity for the datacom optical 
and infrastructure business.

### Page 3

Upward revision to PA foundry TAM reflecting foundry continue to gain
share despite weak smartphone shipment
We have revised up our 2026/27/28E P A foundry TAM by 5/4/2% to
US$1.3bn/1.5bn/1.6bn despite smartphone shipment remaining weak (GSe now at
-10%/+2%/+2% in 2026/27/28, was -10%/+3%/+1% previously, see here) as the
smartphone market remains exposed to the rising memory cost environment (see here).
The upward revision is based on our expectation that PA foundry house will continue to

gain share in the smartphone PA segment as newer smartphone projects from design
houses are being allocated to foundries due to the higher production costs and
increasing design complexity of the PA. This will benefit Win Semi in the mid to long term
(Win Semi’s revenue from cellular business already saw 28% YoY YTD). We now expect
the market TAM to deliver an 8% CAGR over 2026-30E, and we expect foundries’ cellular
PA shipments to increase by 11% in 2026 (Exhibit 2), outperforming our 2026

smartphone shipment estimates of -10% YoY due to the higher than expected PA
foundry penetration (Exhibit 5).
Win Semi delivered 3Q26 revenue that is better than the company’s guidance (actual
QoQ at 31% vs. guidance of low-teens QoQ), with the cellular business in peak season.
Management expects this momentum to slow down in 4Q26 and guided revenue to see
QoQ decline in the cellular business. We now expect Win Semi’s PA foundry revenue to
grow 31% YoY in 2026 (was 28% previously), outpacing the overall PA foundry TAM

growth of 22% YoY, as the company continues to focus on mid-to-high-end and
premium smartphone models (iOS camp accounts for 30-40% of the company’s cellular
business per our estimate), which are relatively more resilient to rising memory costs.
Moreover, we expect Win Semi’s PA foundry market share to decline from 74% in 2020
to 57% in 2026, and further lower to ~50% by 2030, but its revenue will continue to
expand further due to the growing foundry TAM.

We continue to expect Taiwan PA foundry players to see modest recovery in 2026
CAPEX. However, we do not view this as an indication of a recovery in demand for the
overall PA foundry industry. Win Semi maintained its 2026 CAPEX guidance of
NT$2-3bn, with the majority of spending allocated to the new InP and GaN capacity
rather than GaAs capacity expansion. We continue to believe GaAs capacity remains
sufficient as we estimate Win Semi’s quarterly utilization rate could reach at most 85%

throughout 2026-28E (Exhibit 1), suggesting no meaningful need for additional GaAs
capacity expansion.

### Page 5

Investment view 
The company has started to see increasing contribution from the new PD product in 
3Q26 but the contribution remains very limited (overall optical business only saw high 
single digit QoQ in 3Q26), while the company expects PD product revenue to further 
ramp up in 2027 along with more PD capacity coming online. This is tracking more slowly 
than our expectation, and we now expect the datacom optical business to account for 
only a mid-single-digit of 2026E revenue (was expecting high-single-digit previously). 
Meanwhile, progress in new high end LD products (including CW lasers, EML solutions 
and VCSEL) remains broadly the same since last earning call, with the company is still at 
verification stage and expect more meaningful contribution in 2028E. As such, we 
believe Win Semi’s revenue remains highly exposed to the potential weakness in 
smartphone demand, as we estimate smartphone related business (including cellular, 
WiFi, and optical) will account for 50%+ of total revenue in 2026E. The contribution

from datacom optical through 2026-27E could not reach scale, and it remains highly
uncertain whether optical companies will outsource LD products to Win Semi in 2028E.
As a result, we believe Win Semi’s stock price remains too elevated (trading at over 50x
2027E P/E based on GSe/BBG consensus) with limited contribution from the datacom
optical business.
We maintain our conservative view on Win Semi’s stock performance, given (1) the
contribution from datacom optical products (e.g., PDs and LDs) remains limited in

near-term, with the higher margin LD products potentially seeing more contribution in
2028E, (2) the company’s exposure in cellular business remains high (30%+), and the
smartphone market continue to be impacted by high memory cost; and (3) given the
slow technology upgrade speed in the GaAs foundry market, we believe competitors
such as AWSC could catch up with the technology and is better positioned to gain share
given their lower cost structures and more competitive pricing.

Maintain Sell, with a new 12-m TP of NT$162 (from NT$142).

### Page 7

Earnings revisions  
We raise our 2026E revenue by 2% after factor in 3Q26 results and raise our 2027/28E 
revenue by 5/6% to factor in higher infrastructure and optical business contribution. We 
revise up our 2026/27/28E GM estimates by 3.8/2.1/1.6ppt as we expect more margin 
expansion driven by higher UTR and the higher contribution from high margin 
infrastructure business. Overall, we revised up our 2026/27/28E EPS estimates by 
47/6/7%. 
Also, we introduce our 2029E earnings estimates with this report.

Valuation 
We maintain our Sell rating, and revise up our TP from NT$142 to NT$162, based on the 
same target PE of 24x (1x STDV lower than the industry upcycle average valuation), 
factoring in our earnings revisions and, consistent with our group coverage practices, roll 
over our valuation period from 4Q26-3Q27 to 2027E.

Investment Thesis and TP & risks

Win Semi is a key GaAs-based semi foundry player with c.49% global market share as of 
2025. We believe the company’s market share could decline given its much 
higher-than-peer product pricing in the demand downtrend. We also expect the

company to be impacted by the slow PA foundry market growth outlook given weak
end-market demand and high inventory levels. This is despite some longer-term demand

the China PA foundry market, (2) faster-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade

on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trend, and (3) faster-than-expected growth from LEO/AI-related
business.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Summary: Kinsus reported 3Q26 results on 30 October. Net income came in at 
TWD2.39bn (EPS: TWD4.68), 8% below our estimate (TWD2.59bn) but 13% above the 
Bloomberg consensus (TWD2.12bn). Operating results were stronger than expected, 
driven mainly by an improving product mix, including a rising contribution from Nvidia CPU-
related products. While 3Q26 revenue of TWD14.54bn (up 16% QoQ and 40% YoY) was 
largely in line with expectations, gross margin reached 33.9%, 0.7ppt/0.9ppt above our 
estimate/the Bloomberg consensus. Operating margin reached 22.1%, versus our 20.5% 
estimate and the street’s 19.4%, on gross-margin expansion and higher operating 
leverage. However, weaker-than-expected non-operating profit and a higher minority 
interest (TWD373m) versus our estimate (TWD311m) resulted in the 8% net-profit miss 
(vs. our expectations).

Looking ahead, we expect 4Q26 revenue to increase 10% QoQ, driven mainly by ongoing 
price increases and product-mix optimisation, with further gross-margin expansion. We 
believe AMD’s new CPU platform (i.e., Venice) and BT substrates for optical transceivers 
could be the next growth drivers for Kinsus in 2027. Separately, Kinsus’s board approved 
an additional c.TWD60bn project budget for capacity expansion on 30 October, which we 
view as further evidence of tight supply conditions and improving demand visibility. We 
have a Buy (1) rating on Kinsus with a 12M TP of TWD1,580, based on 36x our 1-year-
forward EPS forecast. Key downside risks: weaker-than-expected demand for memory, 
PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take on Kinsus’s 3Q26 results, released on 30 October.

• 3Q26 operating results beat expectations; net profit missed our estimate. Net 
income came in at TWD2.39bn (EPS: TWD4.68), 8% below our estimate (TWD2.59bn) 
but 13% above the Bloomberg consensus (TWD2.12bn). Operating results were 
stronger than expected, driven mainly by an improving product mix, including a rising 
contribution from Nvidia CPU-related products. While 3Q26 revenue of TWD14.54bn (up 
16% QoQ and 40% YoY) was largely in line with expectations, gross margin reached 
33.9%, 0.7ppt/0.9ppt above our estimate/the Bloomberg consensus. Operating margin 
reached 22.1%, versus our 20.5% estimate and the street’s 19.4%, on gross-margin 
expansion and higher operating leverage. However, non-operating profit of TWD36m 
was 59%/72% below our/Bloomberg consensus forecasts, while higher-than-expected 
minority interest of TWD373m (vs. our TWD311m estimate) resulted in the 8% net-profit 
miss (vs. our expectations).

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189 TT) 
30 October 2026 
2

• Business outlook. Looking ahead, we expect 4Q26 revenue to increase 10% QoQ, 
driven mainly by ongoing price increases and product-mix optimisation. We expect 
gross margin to expand further on continued product-mix improvement. We believe 
AMD’s new CPU platform (i.e., Venice) and BT substrates for optical transceivers could 
be the next growth drivers for Kinsus in 2027. Separately, Kinsus’s board approved an 
additional c.TWD59.8bn project budget for capacity expansion on 30 October. This 
follows two project budgets of TWD23.5bn and TWD19.6bn announced in January and 
July 2026, respectively. The three projects would represent an aggregate approved 
budget of c.TWD102.9bn. We view the latest expansion plan as further evidence of tight 
supply conditions and stronger demand visibility.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Kinsus with a 12M TP of TWD1,580, based on a target PER 
of 36x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-
expected demand for memory, PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

長榮航太 (2645 TT)  Trading Buy

預期波音 737 系列交機速度將略為放緩。  
投資建議 ：雖然 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張，但考量
波音將維持 737 系列月均交機一段時間，可能會影響長榮航太
的製造部門復甦情形，故給予長榮航太 Trading Buy 的投資建
議。  
波音 737 系列月交機將維持一段時間，調整長榮航太後續製造
的營運預估： 波音於 10/27/2026 公告 3Q26 財報，並於法說會
上提及 :「當前需維持 737 系列月均交機量穩定在 47 架一段時
間，後續管理階層預期交機提升可能要到 2H27 才會上升至 52
架」，使得長榮航太的製造部門復甦仍需等待一段時間，故研
究部一併調整長榮航太後續的營運預估。  
3Q26 先前的維修漲價陸續貢獻，營運維持高檔： 3Q26 長榮航
太自 2H24 起陸續針對維修業務進行漲價後，加上全球機隊持
續供不應求，增加維修機率與維修產值，惟製造部門雖然波音
已將 737 系列交機上升至 47 架 /月，但短期拉貨力道仍有限，
使得長榮航太的製造業務與轉投資公司營運維持低檔，營運仍
以維修業務為主， 3Q26 營收 52.75 億元， QoQ+1.02% ，預估稅
後純益 8.31 億元， QoQ+10.34% ， EPS 2.22 元。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營
運新動能： 2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機
的逐年擴張之外，在新訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，
預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待後續取得特殊製程認證後，
2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單也有望成為長榮
航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 224.86 億元，
YoY+7.85% ，稅後純益 37.61 億元， YoY+17.06% ，EPS 10.04 元。

3 群益投顧 
圖一、全球商用飛機平均機齡

資料來源 : IATA ，長榮航太  
波音 737 系列月交機將維持一段時間，調整長榮航太後續製造的營運預估：
波音於 10/27/2026 公告 3Q26 財報，並於法說會上提及 :「當前需維持 737 系
列月均交機量穩定在 47 架一段時間，後續管理階層預期交機提升可能要到
2H27 才會上升至 52 架」，使得長榮航太的製造部門復甦仍需等待一段時
間，故研究部一併調整長榮航太後續的營運預估。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營運新動能：  
2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機的逐年擴張之外，在新
訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待
後續取得特殊製程認證後， 2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單
也有望成為長榮航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 224.86 億元，
YoY+7.85% ，稅後純益 37.61 億元， YoY+17.06% ， EPS 10.04 元。  
投資建議：  
雖然 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張，但考量波音將維持 737 系列月
均交機一段時間，可能會影響長榮航太的製造部門復甦情形，故給予長榮航
太 Trading Buy 的投資建議。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/鄭高輯 OWENCHENG@uni-psg.com

結論: 
力成法說會大致符合預期，其中: (1) 3Q26 毛利率略優於預期，稅後
EPS 符合預期 。(2) 4Q26 營收穩健成長 ，UTR 可望持續提升 。(3) FOPLP
缺機台情況有明顯改善，擴產時程不受影響。(4) 2027 年營運將由
FOPLP、FCBGA 等先進封裝驅動成長。由於營運展望與上季變化不大，
大致維持 2026、2027 年獲利預估，預估稅後 EPS 分別為 12.89 元、
18.57 元，考量力成策略佈局以先進封裝廠為定位，可望擺脫記憶體
景氣循環既定印象，故維持PER 20X，目標價 370 元，維持買進建議。

重點摘要: 
1.3Q26 毛利率略優於預期:  
力成 3Q26 營收+9.6%QoQ(+26.9%YoY)， 主要係AI 伺服器帶旺 eSSD 需
求成長 ， 且先進邏輯封裝 、 子公司超豐， 以及Tera Probe/Tera Power
均有雙位數QoQ 成長表現 。 在毛利率方面 ， 較前季提升2.2pt 至24.0%，
略優於預期的 23.5%，反映產品組合優化，且因應原物料成本提升進
行個位數~雙位數%不等的漲價。OPM 亦較前季提升 2.4pt 至 17.7%，
單季稅後 EPS 3.55 元，大致符合預期的 3.60 元。

2.4Q26 穩健成長，維持 2026 年獲利預估不變:  
展望 4Q26，預估營收持續穩定成長，預期 Dram 產能 UTR 維持高檔且
有價格效益，企業級 SSD 需求也將維持高檔，另 AI 相關應用將持續
扮演主要成長引擎，高階 FCBGA 已成功導入大晶片封裝並延續成長動
能，TPJ+TPW 因伺服器及 AI 強勁需求也將保持穩健，相較之下，超豐
在 AI、HPC 及 IoT 需求成長，但消費性電子 、PC 及 NB 拉貨轉趨保守。
目前整體封裝和測試 UTR 都超過 90%，4Q26 將隨營收增加而持續提
升。預估 2026 全年營收+27.9%YoY，稅後 EPS 12.89 元(原估 12.87
元)。

3. FOPLP 缺機台情況有明顯改善，擴產時程不受影響:  
在 FOPLP 進度方面，客戶端產品驗證持續推進，朝向 2027 量產既定
目標穩定推進，且隨著市場需求及量產信心度提升，客戶NPI 的需求
亦顯著增加。在設備交貨狀況方面，儘管半導體廠商對設備需求十分
吃緊，但 在公司與 供應商共同努力下，缺機台情況有明顯改善，
2027~2028 年擴產將按計畫進行，出貨時程不受影響。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

預計 AMD 的 AI 晶片將率先導入 FOPLP， 並於2027 年中以前如期量產，
預估第一階段產能 2K/月。

4.2027 年將由 FOPLP、FCBGA 等先進封裝驅動成長:  
展望 2027 年，力成營運成長將從過去高度仰賴記憶體產品，重塑為
以先進封裝包括 FOPLP、2.5D、3D、矽光 CPO 及高階 FCBGA 所驅動的
成長模式 。 目前先進封裝佔比30%， 預計2027 年在 FOPLP 設備問題改
善，且如期導入 AMD AI 產品下，先進封裝比重將持續提升。此外，
記憶體供需在 2027 年緩和但仍供不應求，在記憶體大廠產能陸續開
出下，有助於記憶體營收穩增並維持高稼動率。預估 2027 年營收
+22.1%YoY， 稅後淨利+45.8%YoY， 稅後EPS 18.57 元(原估 18.25 元)。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下

기아 (000270)

26.3Q Review: 수익성에 대한 입증이 필요  Company Brief

26.3Q Review: 매출액 31.6조원, 영업이익 1.8조원  
기아는 26.3Q 매출액 31.6조원 (+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원 
(+24.3% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, +5.8% OPM) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 26.3Q 영업이익은 
시장 컨센서스 (2.36조원)를 22.9% 하회했다 [iM증권 추정치: 매출액 31.5조원, 
영업이익 2.3조원]. 주요 구성 요소별 내용은 아래와 같다. 
① P: 26.3Q 26.3Q 전동화 차량 판매는 30.0만대 (+47% YoY, 판매 비중 
36.2%)를 기록했는데, 이 중 HEV 17.2 만대 (+46% YoY), EV 12.3 만대 
(+75% YoY)로 구성됨. 미국 HEV 판매가 전년 동기 대비 118% 증가하며 
시장 성장률을 약 4배 상회했고, 특히 서유럽 EV 판매가 전년 대비 163% 
증가함. 단 26.3Q 중 원화가 빠르게 강세로 전환되며 원/달러, 원/유로 
평균 환율은 전분기 대비 각 5.2%, 6.2% 하락함에 따른 – 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매는 84.7만대 (+7.9% YoY) 를 기록했고, 북미, 유럽, 
인도 등 주요 시장에서 고른 성장 나타남. 26.3Q 소매 판매 또한 82.9만대 
(+6.9% YoY)로 글로벌 산업 수요 (-6% YoY)를 상회. 북미는 HEV, 내수와 
유럽은 EV, 인도는 신형 셀토스와 소넷을 중심으로 판매가 확대됨 (이하 
26.3Q 권역별 도매 판매 YoY, 국내: +2.2%, 북미: +7.6%. 유럽: +19.3%. 
인도: +38.5%). 이란 사태 장기화로 아중동 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 
28.4% 감소했으나, 수요가 견조한 중남미와 CIS로 물량을 전환하며 
감소분을 상당 부분 만회함. 연간 도매 판매 가이던스 (335만대)를 유지함. 
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 약 0.7조원으로 추정되며, 전년 동기 대비 
5,000억원 감소함 (25.2Q 0.7조원, 25.3Q 1.2조원, 25.4Q 1.0조원, 26.1Q 
0.8조원). 반면 인센티브 증가 및 가격 인하 7,270억원, 서유럽 EV 비중 
확대에 따른 믹스 악화 4,650억원, 원자재 가격 상승 영향 3,680억원 등 
총 1.6조원의 감익 요인이 발생함. 이에 26.3Q 매출원가율은 84.1%로 
전년 동기 대비 3.0%p 상승함 (인센티브 +1.9%p, 재료비 +1.2%p). 다만 
26.3Q 기말 환율 급락하며 판매보증충당부채 환입 규모가 축소되었고 
판매보증비 약 9,000억원 감소. 판매관리비율은 10.1%로 3.7%p 개선됨. 
26.3Q 기아 순이익은 1.29조원으로 전년 동기 대비 9.6% 감소했다. 원화 강세에 
따라 외환환산 관련 이익이 전년 동기 540억원 이익에서 3,718억원으로 손실 
전환됐고, 현대모비스 등 관계사 이익 감소로 지분법손익 또한 360억원 감소했기 
때문이다 (현대차 순이익 감소 논리와 동일). 1) 산업 수요 감소와 중동 판매 차질에도 
권역별 파워트레인 대응을 통해 그룹사 대비 가지고 있는 외형 성장성을 증명했으나, 
2) 유럽 EV 판매 확대 과정에서 발생한 인센티브와 믹스 악화라는 비용 부담, 원자재 
가격 상승이 관세 감소 효과를 대부분 반납했고, 3) 분기 중 급격한 원화 강세가 
더해지며 시장 기대를 크게 하회하는 수익성을 기록했다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다. 
Buy (Maintain)

기아 (000270)

실적 발표 후 주가 급락: 성장에도 불구하고 수익성에 대한 우려  
26.10.30 실적 발표 당일 기아 주가는 11.7% 하락했다. 하루 전 발표된 그룹사 실적 
발표와 상반된 모습이었다. 시장이 이번 실적에서 확인한 우려는 아래와 같다. 첫째, 
성장과 이익의 괴리다. 관세 영향을 제외한 26.3Q 영업이익은 2.52조원으로 전년 
동기(2.70조원) 대비 오히려 1,770억원 감소했다. 판매 증가에 따른 물량 효과 
(+4,420억원)와 환율 효과 (+5,290억원)가 발생했음에도, 인센티브와 믹스 악화로 이를 
대부분 반납한 것이다. 기아의 외형 성장 상당 부분이 유럽 EV 판매 증가에서 나오는데, 
해당 시장에서는 중국 EV 업체와의 경쟁으로 공격적인 프로모션이 불가피하다. 26.3Q 
유럽 권역에서의 중소형 EV 판매가 사업 계획을 크게 웃돌며 EV 믹스는 13.1%에서 
14.8%로 확대됐다. 시장은 이를 M/S를 위해 수익성을 훼손하고 있다고 받아들이는 
것이다. 둘째, 당장 26.4Q에도 유의미한 수익성 개선이 없을 것이라는 점이다. 기아는 
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 4Q 영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이며, 연초 
제시한 연간 가이던스 (영업이익 10.2조원, +8.3% OPM) 에 다소 미달할 것이라고 
밝혔다. 중동 분쟁의 영향이 최소 연말까지 이어지고, 원자재와 물류비 부담 또한 
지속될 것이 회사의 판단이다. 무엇보다 원/달러 환율이 26.2Q 평균 약 1,505원에서 
26.3Q 약 1,430원, 현재 1,320~1,340원 수준까지 하락하며 이에 따른 - 영향이 커질 
수 있다. 셋째, 그룹사와의 이익 체력 비교다. 현대차는 26.3Q 파업과 생산 차질로 도매 
판매가 11.3% 감소했음에도 원가 절감을 통해 영업이익이 5.5% 증가했고, 시장 
기대를 상회했다. 반면 기아는 판매가 7.9% 증가했음에도 영업이익이 컨센서스를 20% 
이상 하회했다. 26.3Q 양사 영업이익 격차는 약 8,600억원까지 벌어진 상황이다.  
단기 노이즈를 제외한다면 기아의 기업 가치는 여전히 높다  
그럼에도 당사는 기아의 EV 전략, 낮은 수익성을 이유로 중장기 기업 가치에 대한 
기대를 낮출 이유는 없다고 판단한다. 첫째, 외부 요인을 걷어내면 본원적인 수익 
창출력은 유지되고 있다. 기아는 26.3Q 영업이익률을 낮춘 외부 요인을 원자재 1.2%p, 
중동 판매 차질 0.7%p, 환율 1.7%p 등 총 3.6%p로 분석했다. 즉 중동 분쟁이 
해소되면 상당 부분이 사라질 일회성 요인이다. 둘째, EV 수익성 개선 경로는 HEV가 
걸어온 길과 같다. 기아는 HEV 역시 도입 초기에는 수익을 내지 못했으나 현재는 ICE에 
버금가는 수익성을 내고 있다며, 26~27 년 현재의 EV 전략을 유지하겠다고 밝혔다. 
유럽 ICE 시장은 26.3Q 전년 동기 대비 약 20% 축소된 반면 EV 시장은 50% 이상 
성장하고 있다. 전환기에 스케일을 확보하지 못하면 EV 마진을 개선할 방법도 없다. 
기아는 향후 3년간 배터리, 모터, 히트펌프 등 주요 부품에서 연 10% 수준의 원가 
절감을 추진하고, 2~3년 내 EV를 HEV와 같은 수익성 경로에 올려놓겠다는 계획이다. 
셋째, 중장기 성장 계획을 수정하지 않았다. 기아는 27년 사업 계획이 CID 당시 제시한 
숫자와 큰 차이가 없을 것이며, 중장기 영업이익률 10% 목표도 유지했다. IAA 시행에 
대비해 슬로바키아 공장에서 EV를 전량 생산하고 ICE 국내 수출 방안도 검토 중이다. 
주주환원 측면에서도 TSR 35% 를 지키고, 자사주 매입보다 배당을 우선하겠다는 
입장이다. 10/30 종가와 최소 DPS 기준만으로도 배당수익률은 6.3%에 달하고 있다.

기아 판매관리비 (좌) 판매관리비율 (우)
  26.4Q 기아 매출액 31.8조원, 영업이익 2.2조원 전망 
기아 26.4Q 실적을 매출액 31.8조원 (+13.2% YoY), 영업이익 2.2조원 (+20.8% YoY, 
+7.0% OPM)으로 전망한다. 26.4Q 판매 성장세는 이어지고, 3Q에 집중 반영된 원자재 
부담 일부 완화와 계절적 성수기 효과로 3Q 대비 수익성은 소폭 개선될 것이다. 다만 
1) 원화 강세에 따른 평균 환율 하락, 2) 유럽 EV 인센티브의 높은 수준 유지 (미국은 
할부 금리 상승으로 QoQ 약 200달러 증가, 유럽은 전분기와 유사), 3) 중동 판매 
차질과 원자재, 물류비 부담 지속을 감안하면 의미 있는 반등은 어렵다. 이는 4Q 
영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이라는 회사의 언급과도 궤를 같이한다. 
기아 목표주가 150,000원으로 하향  
기아 목표주가를 26.4Q 추정치 하향을 반영해 150,000원으로 하향한다. 기아의 판매 
경쟁력은 금번 분기에도 증명됐다. 다만 EV 판매 확대가 수익성 개선으로 연결된다는 
점이 숫자로 확인되기 전까지, 시장은 기아의 성장에 할인율을 적용할 것이다. 중동 
분쟁 해소와 EV 원가 절감이 맞물리는 27년이 기아가 수익성을 증명할 시점이 될 
것이며, 6%를 상회하는 배당수익률이 그때까지 주가의 하방을 지지할 것으로 판단된다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임

기아 (000270)

Company Brief 
자동차/모빌리티 
Analyst 김준성 
joonsung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 배현서 
hyeonseo.bae@meritz.co.kr 배당은 지켜질 것  
3Q26 지배주주 순이익, 컨센서스 -38% 하회 
매출 31.6조원 (+10% YoY) 으로 컨센서스에 부합했다. 출고 판매 (+10% YoY), 소
매 판매 (+8% YoY) 모두 높은 성장을 기록하며, 외형 확대에 기여했다. 
영업이익 1.82조원 (+24% YoY) 으로 컨센서스를 -25% 하회했다. 전년 동기 대미 
관세 25% 부과에 따른 기저효과 +5,330억원 (2025년 11월부터 15%로 하향)이 
존재했고 기말 환율 (원/달러 -12% QoQ 강세) 변동에 따른 판매보증 충당부채 환
입 +6,700억원을 인식했음에도 불구하고, 신규 출시 저가 BEV 모델들의 판매 확대
에 따른 전사 평균 판매가격 하락  할인 경쟁 심화  원재료 및 운송비 상승으로 부
진한 실적을 기록했다. 
지배주주 순이익 1.29조원 (-10% YoY) 으로 컨센서스를 -38% 하회했다. 영업 실적 
부진과 함께 원화 강세에 의한 외화자산 평가손실 및 관계사 실적 악화에 따른 지분
법 이익 감소 (-36% YoY)가 더해진 결과다.

6,000원 이상 배당 유지 전망 
4Q26 이후 실적 방향도 먹구름이 가득하다. 원/달러 환율의 YoY 강세 반영이 시작
된다. 미국 시장 내 경쟁력 확보를 위해 가장 중요한 원/엔 환율의 방향성 또한 비우
호적 흐름이 지속 중이다. 내수 시장을 포함해 전세계 주요 시장 내 중국산 차량과의 
경쟁 강도도 높아지고 있다. 원유 가격 뿐만 아니라 알루미늄, 구리 등 핵심 원재료 
가격의 상승 반영도 이어지고 있다. 
2026년 연간 지배주주 순이익 6.9조원 (-9% YoY) 을 전망한다. 주주환원 총액은 축
소될 것이다. 다만 자사주 매입을 줄이더라도 배당은 전년 수준을 지킬 것으로 기대
한다. 참고로 2025년 실적에 기반한 주주환원은 자사주 매입 0.9조원, 배당 2.6조원 
(주당 6,800원)이었으며, 주주환원율은 48%였다. 현대차그룹은 Boston Dynamics
에 대한 증자를 이어가고 있다. 정의선 회장도 마찬가지다. 그리고 기아의 배당은 현
대차  현대글로비스의 배당과 함께 증자 재원으로 활용되고 있다. 6,000 원 이상의 
배당 유지를 기대하며, 6% 이상 시가 배당 수익률 제시를 통해 주가 하방 경직성 확
보가 가능하다고 판단한다. 기업가치 상승 재개를 위해서는 기존 차량 제조  판매 사
업을 넘어 피지컬 데이터 디바이스 제공 사업 진출 가시성 확보가 필요하다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 11. 2

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 150,000원 
현재주가 (10.30) 95,600원 
상승여력 56.9%

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 방위산업 | 2026. 11. 2  
방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감

방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감 
지상방산 매출 1조 2,450억원으로 전년비 41%, 영업이익 217억원으로 전년비 
96.2% 급감하며 영업이익률은 1.7% 기록. 실적 급감에 대해 사측은 올해 주요 사업
의 납품 일정이 4분기에 집중됨에 따라 3분기 인도 물량이 적었던 것과 대전 사업장 
생산 중단에 따른 영향 때문이라고 설명. 단, 4분기에는 당초 계획에 따라 주요 사업의 
납품 물량이 집중되어 있어 실적이 개선될 것이고 대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 내에 정
상 가돌 하는 것이 목표라고 밝힘 
수주잔고 증가세 지속으로 성장 지속 가능할 것 
3분기 말 수주잔고 35조원으로 전년비 12.9% 증가. 이를 통한 지상방산 부문 성장 
지속 가능할 것으로 판단. 참고로 사측은 내년 매출 15% 수준 성장 전망 유지. 하지
만 금리 상승에 따른 글로벌 업종 평균 밸류에이션 하락에 따른 방산 부문 적용 멀티
플 하향 조정 그리고 상장 연결회사(한화오션, 한화시스템, 쎄트렉아이)와 상장 비연결
회사(한국항공우주)의 주가 하락 반영 영향으로 목표주가는 하향 조정 
8월 미 육군 차륜형 자주포 사업에서 시제 사업자로 선정. 향후 K9MH는 미 육군의 
작전 운용 개념에 맞춰 맞춤 조정을 거친 후에 특별한 결함 혹은 결격 사유가 없으면 
최종 계약 체결 예정. 동시에 미 육군의 궤도형 자주포 사업에도 참여할 계획. 뿐만 아
니라 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등으로 신시장 개척 역시 지속되고 있다는 점도 긍
정적 
 Analyst 이재광 
rheejaekwang @lssec.co.kr

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82만원에서 64만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

COMPLIANCE NOTICE 
작성자(한승한)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다.

Document 328: Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

13023135     1

Economy 
Entering the age of the older consumer 
01 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• The older consumer is moving center stage. America's population has been aging for decades, but the shift has accelerated: the 
share aged over 60 increased by almost 10 percentage points (pp) between 1995 and 2025. And the Census Bureau projects the 
share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population. 
• More free time means a different spending mix. People aged 65 and over spend around two and a half fewer hours per day 
working and roughly two hours more on leisure than the overall adult population. Bank of America data shows that older 
households allocate relatively more of their card spending to groceries and travel, and less to restaurants, gasoline, general 
merchandise and clothing. 
• Households aged over 55 held close to $140 trillion in net worth in the second quarter of 2026, giving many older consumers 
considerable capacity to spend on travel and other leisure categories. But wealth is not evenly distributed, and healthcare and 
other cost pressures remain significant for older households on tighter or fixed incomes.

Population aging is nothing new, but it is accelerating  
The US population has been growing relatively “older” for a long time. Exhibit 1 shows that the proportion of the population 
under 20 years old shrank from the mid-1960s through 2025. At the same time, the proportion of the population in older age 
groups has been rising.  
Exhibit 1: Over time the US population has steadily aged  
Proportion of US population by age range (%)

Source: Human Mortality Database; Bank of America Institute calculations 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

### Page 2

2 01 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Additionally, there has been a notable acceleration in the relative aging of the population in recent years. For example, between 
1995 and 2025, the proportion of the population aged over 60 rose by almost 10 percentage points (pp) to 25%.  
Several factors have driven this. First, fertility rates in the US, as in much of the world, have fallen sharply. This has slowed 
natural population growth and, by reducing the number of younger people relative to older cohorts, has increased the share of 
the population at older ages. Second, more recently, net inward migration to the US has slowed. Because migrants tend to be 
younger than the overall population, weaker immigration also tends to raise the average age. Finally, rising life expectancy over 
much of the post-war period has allowed more people to survive into older age, further increasing the older population's share.  
Exhibit 2: The trend towards an older composition of the population is expected to continue  
Forecast changes in the share of population by age group relative to 2025 (pp)

Source: United States Census Bureau 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Lower fertility and, more recently, weaker net migration look set to slow overall US population growth in the decades ahead. As a 
result, older Americans are likely to play an increasingly important role in the economy, both as a share of the population and, in 
many age groups, in absolute numbers. The Census Bureau projects the share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 
2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population (Exhibit 2). For businesses, understanding how the needs and spending patterns 
of older consumers differ from those of younger generations will therefore become increasingly relevant. 
Older age groups allocate their time differently  
Why does this matter? One reason is that older people tend to spend their time and money quite differently from younger adults. 
Most obviously, many work less or have retired altogether. Data from the American Time Use Survey (ATUS) shows that people 
aged 65 and over spend, on average, around two and a half fewer hours per day working than the population aged 15+ (Exhibit 
3). 
That leaves considerably more time for other activities. People aged 65 and over spend around two hours more per day on 
leisure and sports activities, with television viewing accounting for a particularly large share of this additional leisure time 
(Exhibit 4).

Older households also spend differently  
Bank of America internal data on credit and debit card spending provides insights into how these differences in older 
generations’ use of time also impact their spending. Rather than splitting our spending data by generation, as we often do, here 
we split by age bands, allowing for consistent comparisons over time.  
Exhibit 5 shows how the composition of card spending of households aged 61-75 and those older than 75 differs from that of all 
households. A few things stand out. For one, older households devote a greater share of their overall card spending to groceries 
(food and beverages). Households aged 61-75 also allocate more spending to travel (airlines and lodging). And restaurants and 
bars, gasoline, general merchandise and clothing account for a smaller share of older households’ card spending.  
These differences may seem fairly intuitive. Older households have more leisure hours, so spend relatively more on travel to 
enjoy them. Previously we found that more available time may support travel particularly around retirement, although the 
discretionary spending lift can be temporary (for more, read our publication: Paychecks to pensions: The evolution of retiree 
spending).  
Older households are also likely to have less need to update their work wardrobes, so they probably spend less on clothing.

Still, older households’ relatively lower spending on restaurants and bars is a little more curious, perhaps, given dining out could 
be one way of making use of more leisure time. Indeed, when we look at how these spending shares are evolving, we find a 
nuanced picture. While households aged 61-75 do spend relatively less on restaurants and bars than the overall population, they 
appear to be narrowing this gap. But for those older than 75 this is not the case. In our view, the pandemic may still be weighing 
on dining-out spending among this older age group (Exhibit 6).

Aging presents opportunities, but also challenges 
In our view, the aging of the US population presents important opportunities for the economy and businesses. Older consumers 
have more leisure time and businesses that cater to these demands may therefore benefit. 
Moreover, some older consumers hold considerable amounts of net wealth and have been some of the biggest beneficiaries of 
recent rises in equities. Using data from the Federal Reserve, Exhibit 8 shows that as of the second quarter of 2026 the net 
worth of those households aged 55 and over was close to $140 trillion, around three-quarters of the total. And with equity 
markets rising strongly, these age groups have seen a more than 20% increase in their net worth over the past two years, 
potentially enhancing their spending power further.  
But an important caveat is that not all older consumers are in this position. Previous Institute work found evidence consistent 
with equity-market wealth effects being concentrated toward higher-income households (read our publication: Paychecks to 
pensions: The evolution of retiree spending). And Census Bureau research has found that around 14% of Social Security 
recipients aged over 65 rely on it for over 90% of their income. For older households without a wealth cushion, recent elevated 
cost pressures, like gasoline, will likely have put them under more significant financial strain. They will also be more dependent 
on cost-of-living (COLA) increases in Social Security to underpin their spending.  
And, more broadly, outside of card spending, healthcare costs are a much larger factor in older households’ budgets (Exhibit 9). 
The evolution of these costs going forward will be a key variable for older consumers’ budgets.

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
The differences between the total and per household card spending growth rate (if discussed) can be explained by the following reasons:  
1. Overall total card spending growth is partially boosted by the growth in the number of active cardholders in our sample. This could be due to 
an increasing customer base or inactive customers using their cards more frequently. 
2. Per household card spending growth only looks at households that complete at least five transactions with Bank of America cards in the 
month. Per household spending growth isolates impacts from a changing sample size, which could be unrelated to underlying economic 
momentum, and potential spending volatility from less active users. 
3. Overall total card spending includes small business card spending while per household card spending does not. 
4. Differences due to using processing dates (total card spending) versus transaction date (per household card spending). 
5. Other differences including household formations due to young adults moving in and out of their parent’s houses during COVID. 
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
We consider a measure of services necessity spending that includes but is not limited to groceries, utilities, and gasoline. Discretionary services 
includes but is not limited to charitable donations, leisure travel, entertainment, and professional/consumer services. Discretionary retail 
includes but is not limited to general merchandise, miscellaneous, clothing, electronics and furniture. It excludes categories like groceries and 
gasoline. Holiday spending is defined as items in which spending in the November-December period is usually at least 20% of total annual 
spending on the category.  Value and premium grocers are determined judgmentally on a proprietary analysis of merchants by equity analysts.  
Durables spending is defined as spending on electronics, building materials, auto-related spending and furniture. Premium durables spending is 
based on a selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively higher value products. Conversely, value durables spending is based on a 
selection of retailers who are judged to sell relatively lower value products.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

与低碳燃料 5 项（可再生能源合成氨、农林生物质气化制甲醇、农林

塑料化学再生、厨余垃圾集中厌氧处理沼气工程）。

年底，我国已发布 CCER 方法学 18 个，分别是造林碳汇、并网光热

回收利用、淤地坝碳汇、滨海盐沼植被修复、海草床植被修、规模化
猪场粪污沼气回收利用工程、农业废弃物集中处理工程、既有公共建
筑围护结构与供暖通风空调系统能效提升、可再生能源电解水制氢、
电气设备六氟化硫回收和净化、中深层地热能井下换热供暖。

大型活动碳中和、企业环境社会治理、生态修复等多样场景。2024 年
1 月，全国温室气体自愿减排交易市场正式启动。

ESG 与可持续发展周报
2024 年启动标准前期研究，联合青海大学等科研单位，组建标准编制
组，推进标准研究制定。截至目前，依托绿电溯源成果，青海已建成
全省 992 家企业的“碳账户”，有效支撑“碳账户”贷款发放，推动
绿色金融精准服务低碳企业。
（2）“十五五”时期，全国规划建成 500 个零碳工厂，同时将非化石
能源电力消费物理认定量占比目标提升至 35%。这一政策背景让零碳
工厂成为新型储能最具确定性的应用场景之一。业内人士认为，在政
策硬约束驱动下，储能正从过去单一的峰谷调节工具，升级为支撑绿
电消纳、算电协同、碳资产管理与源网荷储一体化的关键枢纽，配套
储能设施也从工厂建设的可选项加速转变为必选项。与此同时，经济
性瓶颈、长时储能技术缺口、精细化运营能力不足，以及绿色供应链
的国际合规挑战，仍是储能全面落地工厂场景必须跨越的坎。
 技术前沿
《2026 全球碳中和年度进展报告》（以下简称《报告》）近日发布。
《报告》指出，2010—2024 年，全球光伏降本中超过六成、风电降本

中超过八成来自我国，我国风光成本下降对全球产生溢出效应。当
前，我国已形成低碳技术的全球领先优势。2025 年我国新增可再生能
源装机 440 吉瓦，约占全球的 64%，风光度电成本居全球最低区间；
纯电动车销量 820 万辆，保有量占全球的 58.9%。在新兴技术领域，
绿 氢新 增 产能 占 全球 的 70% ， 新型 储 能装 机 136 吉 瓦、 同 比增 长
84%，碳捕集、利用与封存（CCUS）运行项目 20 个、居全球第二，
五年森林生物质碳储量增加约 11.5 亿吨。
《报告》指出，全球低碳转型正进入由技术驱动、系统条件决定的新
阶段。与此同时，风光储、电动车等成熟技术加速发展，但电网消纳
能力、融资、市场机制等方面存在的不足仍制约着市场替代；清洁
氢、CCUS 等新兴技术仍面临需求、基础设施和商业模式不足的问
题。
 研究探索
10 月 20 日，在由中国金融四十人论坛（CF40）与清华大学（THU）
联合主办的 2026 外滩年会上，2026 外滩绿色金融报告（下称报告）

正式发布。本次报告主题为“绿色低碳协同发展及金融支持研究”。
中国人民银行研究局局长、CF40 成员王信担任课题负责人。报告跳出
“就绿色论绿色”的传统视角，提出“协同发展”应对多重目标、多
元风险叠加复杂格局的路径：第一层是绿色低碳发展自身的协同，包
括气候减缓与气候适应的协同、应对气候变化与自然生态保护的协
同；第二层是绿色低碳转型与国内宏观经济金融的协同；第三层是国
际视角下的协同。
报告选取农业、城市、健康医疗三个代表性领域，测算“十五五”期
间气候适应投资规模。预计“十五五”期间，农业领域气候适应投资
总需求约 1.05 万亿元，年均约 2100 亿元；39 个深化气候适应型城市
（区）建设试点地区气候适应总投资需求约 3000 亿至 4000 亿元，年
均约 600 亿至 800 亿元，扩展到全国，年均投资需求规模约 4000 亿
至 5400 亿元；健康医疗领域气候适应投资总需求约 1.88 万亿元，年
均约 3800 亿元。绿色消费方面，2025 年我国绿色消费市场规模达

12.18 万亿元，占社零总额 24.3%。报告预计，2030 年我国绿色消费
将突破 18 万亿元，占比提升至 30%左右。
王信表示，需要完善高级别政策协同机制，逐步把气候、自然等因素
纳入国民经济发展战略、财政管理框架、货币政策框架以及宏观审慎

### Page 3

ESG 与可持续发展周报
管理框架。同时完善并落地可持续金融标准，根据试用情况完善转型
金融标准、生物多样性金融标准以及蓝色金融标准。
 国际合作
今年的联合国气候变化大会（COP31）将于 11 月 9 日至 20 日在土耳
其安塔利亚举行，围绕《联合国气候变化框架公约》及其《巴黎协
定》实施涉及的减缓、适应、资金、公正转型等近百项议题开展谈判
磋商。
生态环境部副部长李高介绍，他将再次担任中国代表团团长。生态环
境部将会同外交部等十多个部委、研究机构，以及香港、澳门特别行
政区政府，共同组成中国代表团参会。
气候变化是全球共同面临的严峻挑战。中国是《联合国气候变化框架
公约》最早的 10 个缔约方之一。
李高表示，目前全球气候治理的重心已经从规则谈判转向行动落实。
中方支持 COP31 聚焦“落实”，大会应深入讨论发达国家减排力
度、资金支持不足的“老问题”和单边主义、保护主义损害应对气候
变化集体努力的“新挑战”。
 投资建议
我们认为，全国温室气体自愿减排交易市场支持领域持续扩张，对后

续绿色金融市场将会带来显著推动。在政策端不断落地生效带来正向
动力的同时，各部门也在积极推动绿色经济的不断推进，建议关注绿
色金融相关行业。

► 隨人工智慧運算需求持續攀升 ，谷歌、亞馬
遜、臉書、微軟及OpenAI等企業巨頭積極發
展客製化晶片 (ASIC)，藉此提升運算效率並
優化成 本 。根據 凱 基投顧 引述產 業數 據預
估，2027年客製化晶片出貨量將年增 92%至
1,446萬顆 ，首度超 越繪圖處理器 (GPU)的
1,129萬顆 ，顯示AI晶片市場正由 GPU主導
逐步轉向多元化發展。
► 在客製化晶片方面 ，谷歌持續擴大自研張量
處理器 (TPU)的應用與對外供應 ，亞馬遜亦
積極拓展自研AI晶片(Trainium)的雲端運算服
務，預估兩者合計將佔 2027年客製化晶片出
貨量約80%。
► 隨客製 化晶 片需 求 快速增 加 ，相 關晶 片設
計、先 進製 程及 先 進封裝 需求可 望同 步提
升，涉及上述業務發展的台股供應鏈 (註)有機
會受到市場關注。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 2024年全球工廠運作的工業機器人約 470萬
台，為10年前的 3倍，主要為韓國 、中國和
日本等製造業大國將其運用於汽車 、電子和
金屬加工等產業 ，並因而維持成本和品質優
勢，為競爭力重要來源之一 。雖然美國工業
機器人的普及程度仍低於亞洲主要製造業國
家，但近年積極尋求重建工業產能 、將關鍵
供應鏈遷回美國國內以及擴大半導體和製藥
生產，有可能讓美國逐步調整工業和製造層
面的策略方向。
► 傳統工業機器人與人形機器人在外觀設計與
應用場景上有明顯差異 。工業機器人通常部
署於高度標準化的環境 ，以執行範圍相對有
限且重複性高的特定作業為主 ，而人形機器
人則被設計用於多變的環境 ，並經常需要與
人類協同工作 ，因此需結合移動能力 、感知
能力與靈巧操作能力 ，才能夠執行多樣化任
務，而非受限單一功能。
工業機器人技術相對成熟且部署速度較快全球運作中的工業機器人數量穩定成長
工業機器人技術成熟且穩定成長，為製造業強國的重要競爭力來源

### Page 7

投資焦點人形機器人
► 人 形機 器 人 主 要由 結 合半 導 體 與AI 模 型的
「大腦」，並由感測器 、執行器 、線材、連
接器及輕量化電池驅動的機身所組成 ，可透
過感知環境資訊 、思考並進行協調的實體動
作。投行預估至2030年，執行器將佔整體人
形機器人成本超過 50%，為價值含量最高的
核心零組件 ，麥肯錫公司甚至認為執行器與
感測器可能成為未來供應鏈中的關鍵瓶頸。
► 機器人在實體世界中執行任務時 ，任何延遲
或連線中斷都可能帶來重大風險 ，因此相較
純數位的 AI應用 ，對即時運算 (邊緣運算 )能
力的要求更高 。而近幾年邊緣運算軟硬體能
力大幅提升 ，使本地端設備得以部署數十億
參數的 AI模型 ，並將反應延遲縮短至約 0.3
秒，使機器人能夠即時感知 、決策與行動 。
此外，過去6年風險資本對機器人、感測器及
IT 硬體的每年平均投資約 100 億美元 ， 但
2026年僅上半年即已達 280億美元 ，暗示市
場對機器人產業潛力的期待有別於過往。
機器人技術進展快速並開始吸引風險資本投資人形機器人運作有賴AI模型與大量機身零組件整合

人形機器人產業結合AI與硬體，含金量高吸引風險資本進駐

### Page 8

8
股市
◆ 美、歐、日央行升息抑通膨 ，公債殖利率走高壓抑股債市場近期動能 。然而包括油價自高檔回落使
通膨壓力稍緩 ，以及美股第三季獲利年增率仍為雙位數成長 ，股市表現有支撐 。市場持續觀察AI資
本支出是否帶來獲利成長 ，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場動盪 ，可分散配置於不同國家與產業 ，降
低市場波動影響 。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評價面壓力等紛擾帶來負面影響 ，可布局防
禦股抗震，長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
◆ 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
◆ 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示 ，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間 ，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展 ，加上9月製造業及服務業PMI優於預期，殖利率偏高環境建議

以中短天期債券投資為主 ，可逢利率走升時布局進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流
與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
◆ 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
◆ 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
◆ 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
◆ 中東地緣衝突加劇推升油價，短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
◆ 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法

### Page 15

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 2: From potential to progress 
02 November 2026

Key takeaways 
• In the second and final part of our quantum dictionary series, we move beyond the basics to explore where quantum could go 
next. Today's quantum computers remain limited by noise and errors, but their potential grows rapidly as they scale. Emerging 
applications like quantum AI and sensing could eventually unlock new capabilities and significant economic value. 
• Quantum communication could change both how we connect quantum computers and how we protect information. A quantum 
internet could use entanglement to link quantum systems, creating additional computing power, while quantum networks could 
make eavesdropping detectable and offer new ways to securely transmit information. 
• Meanwhile, the race for quantum leadership is already underway. Governments are pursuing greater quantum sovereignty as the 
technology's potential implications for competitiveness, security and defense grow.

Assessing quantum’s performance and progress 
Quantum advantage versus supremacy 
Quantum advantage is reached when a quantum computer can solve a useful, real-world problem faster than a classical 
computer. The problem is, today’s quantum computers are still too noisy, as we discussed in part one of this series, and qubit 
quality isn’t yet high enough to demonstrate quantum advantage.  
Quantum supremacy takes this one step further. It refers to the point when a quantum computer has computational advantage, 
meaning it can solve a problem that a traditional computer couldn’t in any feasible amount of time.  
Quantum scaling 
Quantum scaling is about building bigger, more powerful quantum computers. It involves overcoming the challenges of 
increasing both the number and the quality of qubits. Today, we’re in the noisy intermediate-scale quantum era. Quantum 
computers have tens to a few hundred qubits, but noise and errors still limit what they can do.1 
However, when quantum computers do scale, they do so exponentially. Every additional qubit doubles the number of operations 
the system can perform. A quantum computer with just 10 qubits could perform more than 100 times the operations of a 
classical computer (Exhibit 1). More qubits could therefore mean more operations and, ultimately, more economic value in the 
industries where quantum computing can be put to work, according to BofA Global Research. 
Exhibit 1: A quantum computer with just 10 qubits could perform more than 100 times the operations than classical 
Number of classical versus quantum operations for a given (qu)bit number (log scale)

Quantum Benchmarking Initiative 
The Defense Advanced Research Projects Agency’s (DARPA’s) Quantum Benchmarking Initiative (QBI) aims to answer a big 
question: Is it possible to build a commercially relevant quantum computer by 2033? To be clear, QBI is not a competition 
between companies. DARPA evaluates all viable approaches for which funding is available.  
QBI has three stages:  
• Stage A: Start with a concept. At this stage, there is a utility-scale quantum computer concept that has a plausible path to 
realization in the near term. 
• Stage B: Put a plan behind it. This stage involves developing a research and development plan, identifying the associated 
risks and introducing prototypes to overcome them. 
• Stage C: Put it to the test. In the final stage, companies work with the government to verify and validate that the utility-
scale quantum computer concept can be built as intended. 
Exploring emerging quantum applications  
Quantum AI 
According to BofA Global Research, quantum AI brings together two powerful technologies: AI and quantum computing, with the 
potential to improve both the physical and the digital worlds. And the relationship could work both ways. Quantum computing 
could provide some of the compute power needed to train generative AI, while generative AI could help speed up the 
development and testing time of quantum systems.  
AI tools like machine learning (ML) could also make quantum error correction (QEC) more efficient and scalable.2 ML models 
trained on simulated datasets can identify and correct errors faster than traditional methods while using fewer computing 
resources. They can also more accurately predict errors before they happen.3 
Quantum sensing 
Quantum sensing uses the properties of quantum mechanics to measure things like magnetic fields, gravitational forces, time 
and rotation with greater precision than classical instruments (Exhibit 2).4 
Exhibit 2: Quantum sensors use quantum properties to measure physical quantities with unmatched precision 
An infographic of quantum sensing

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

2 Wang, Z. & Tang, H. (2024, December 29). Artificial Intelligence for Quantum Error Correction: A Comprehensive Review. arXiv. 
3 Swayne, M. (2025, January 6). AI for Quantum Error Correction: A Comprehensive Guide to Using Artificial Intelligence to Improve Quantum Error Correction. 
Quantum Insider.  
4 Rehman, M.U. (2026, March 2). Understanding Quantum Sensing and Its Industrial Potential. Quantum Insider.

### Page 3

02 November 2026    3

I NSTI TUTE

So, how does it work? In simple terms, there are three steps. First, a quantum system is prepared in a well-defined initial state, 
for example, by cooling an atom to near absolute zero. Next, the sensor is exposed to whatever is being measured, causing a 
subtle shift in its quantum state. In an atomic magnetometer, for example, a magnetic field changes the energy levels of an 
atom’s electrons, which changes how the atom absorbs or emits light. Finally, the sensor measures how much the quantum state 
has shifted, revealing the strength of the external force.5  
Benefits and challenges 
Compared with quantum computers, quantum sensors face some lower technical barriers. They don’t need nearly as many qubits 
to be useful, entanglement can help but isn’t required for every application, and even short coherence times can produce useful 
measurements.6 The catch? They often require large vacuum chambers, laser systems and magnetic shielding to maintain 
coherence. 
The quantum internet and communications  
Quantum communication 
Quantum communication is the study of how information can be encoded and transmitted between quantum systems over a 
distance (Exhibit 3). It uses properties like superposition and entanglement to enable the secure transmission of information. 
Why does that matter? One of the main goals is to keep information secure from potential eavesdroppers, particularly as more 
powerful quantum computers could pose new challenges for traditional communication methods. The ultimate goal is what some 
call the quantum internet, where quantum signals can move seamlessly across many quantum networks.7 
Exhibit 3: Quantum communication uses quantum states to encode and transmit information 
An infographic of quantum communication

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Quantum internet 
Think of the quantum internet as a scalable network that allows two or more quantum devices to exchange information (Exhibit 
4). These connections could span vastly different distances, from within a silicon chip all the way to satellite links.8 
But the quantum internet isn’t meant to replace the internet we use today, nor is it simply a faster version of it. According to 
Cisco, the classical internet can move information between computers, but it is ultimately limited by what those computers can 
compute. Some problems become so complex so quickly that no classical machine can solve them in a useful amount of time.

5 Ibid.  
6 Ibid.  
7 Quantinuum. (2024, July 30). Coming Over the Horizon: Quantum Communication Enters the Mainstream. 
8 Imperial. (n.d.). Quantum Internet.

### Page 4

4 02 November 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

When connection becomes computation 
Here’s what makes the quantum internet different: the connection itself can create new computing power. Using entanglement, 
a quantum network can connect quantum systems into a single, larger computing system that scales exponentially.9 That’s 
fundamentally different from classical computers, which communicate by passing copies of information back and forth. Quantum 
computers connected through a quantum network can instead create a feedback loop of entanglement and a shared space of 
possibilities that neither could produce on its own. 
Different hardware from classical 
Building a quantum internet requires a different set of hardware than the routers and switches that power today’s internet. Key 
pieces include:10   
• Quantum communication channels: These carry quantum signals, typically through fiber-optic cables or satellite links 
using free-space optics. 
• Quantum memory: To keep the network in sync, the system must catch a photon and store its quantum state in a 
stationary atom without destroying it. 
• Quantum repeaters: These help quantum signals travel longer distances by amplifying signals and reducing error rates. 
According to QuEra, the lack of viable quantum repeaters is currently a key limitation to building a global quantum 
network. 
Exhibit 4: A network of quantum computers which could send and receive information encoded in quantum states 
An infographic explaining the quantum internet

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Quantum networking 
Quantum networking is a way of sending information using quantum states. It generally includes three network types: quantum 
key distribution (QKD) for secure communications (more on this below), the quantum internet for distributed quantum 
computing, and quantum sensing networks that use entanglement to improve measurements.  
Classical networks turn information into electrical or optical signals that can be copied or intercepted without the sender or 
receiver knowing. Quantum networks work differently. Because information is transmitted using quantum states, it cannot be

9 Chan, M. (2026, June 1). Quantum internet 101: What it is, and why it matters. Cisco. 
10 QuEra. (n.d.) Quantum Internet.

### Page 5

02 November 2026    5

I NSTI TUTE

intercepted and copied without disturbing the original information. That disturbance makes an eavesdropping attempt 
detectable. 
Evaluating security & strategic risks 
Quantum encryption 
Quantum encryption uses the principles of quantum mechanics, including superposition and entanglement, to encrypt and 
securely transmit data (Exhibit 5). 
One potential risk of quantum computing is that it could eventually break widely used forms of encryption. And while large-scale 
quantum computers are still being developed, organizations may need to start preparing for that possibility today.  
Exhibit 5: Examples include quantum key distribution and post-quantum cryptography 
An infographic illustrating quantum encryption

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Quantum key distribution 
Quantum key distribution (QKD), a type of quantum encryption, uses the laws of quantum physics to create and distribute secure 
keys which prevent data decryption.11 Think of a key as something that locks and unlocks a digital message. Two parties share a 
key to encrypt and decrypt information sent between them. If an eavesdropper tries to obtain the key, the attempt disturbs the 
quantum states, alerting the parties that the key may have been compromised and shouldn’t be used.12 
According to Quantum Insider, QKD generally involves three steps:13 
• Create entangled photons: First, a quantum source, such as a laser and nonlinear crystal or a quantum dot, generates pairs 
of entangled photons. The photons are correlated in ways that classical light cannot replicate, so measurements of one 
reveal information about its partner.  
• Send the photons: The photons travel to different locations through fiber-optic cables or free-space optical channels, 
such as satellite-to-ground or satellite-to-satellite links, while maintaining their quantum correlations. But those 
connections can be lost over long distances, with current systems reaching roughly 100 kilometers, according to Quantum 
Insider.14 Classical networks get around distance limitations with repeaters that receive, amplify and retransmit signals.

11 Toshiba. (n.d.). Quantum Key Distribution (QKD). 
12 QuEra. (n.d.). Quantum Key Distribution. 
13 Rehman, M.U. (2026, March 9). Understanding Quantum Networking and Its Industrial Potential. Quantum Insider. 
14 Ibid.

### Page 6

6 02 November 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Quantum signals can’t simply be amplified without destroying their quantum properties, so quantum repeaters take a 
different approach. They use entanglement swapping and quantum memory to extend the range of quantum networks. 
• Measure the photons: Finally, the photons are measured at each location. This confirms whether the entanglement 
survived the journey and can reveal whether someone tried to intercept the photons along the way. The results can then 
be used to generate a shared secret key for encrypting classical messages. 
Quantum sovereignty 
Quantum sovereignty is an economic actor’s ability to develop, produce and operate its own quantum technology.  
Quantum is the first new computing platform technology since the arrival of digital computing. And the stakes are high. 
Stakeholders are interested in quantum because of its potential implications for economic competitiveness, security, supply 
chain resilience and defense. As a result, some may pursue “sovereign quantum,” meaning quantum capabilities are owned and 
operated by a particular nation. That could involve investing in quantum technology, building domestic expertise or even export 
controls to limit other stakeholders’ ability to develop their own sovereign capabilities.  
The geopolitical race is on 
Government quantum investment has reached at least about $58 billion cumulatively as of 2025, led by Europe at $15.4 billion 
and China at $15.3 billion.  
The competition extends beyond investment. At least nine countries implemented export controls on quantum technology in 
2024. Over the past 20 years, China and the US have led in patent requests filed, but Europe and China have led in the number 
of patents granted. The race for talent is global, too. China has trained the largest share of top quantum researchers at the 
undergraduate level, at roughly one-fourth, while the US is home to the largest share of top talent by employment, at one-third.  
Quantum supply chain 
Building quantum technology requires much more than qubits. A broader supply chain provides the equipment, components and 
materials needed to make quantum systems work, including: 
• Cryogenics: Most types of qubits require some level of extreme cooling, with superconducting qubits among the most 
demanding at roughly 5–10 millikelvin. Keeping qubits this cold helps shield them from environmental noise that can 
cause decoherence. 
• Qubit control electronics: Quantum gates direct qubits by sending them photons. That requires supporting electronics, 
including wiring, room-temperature electronics and amplifiers. For example, analog microwave signals used to read qubits 
must be amplified through several stages before they can be converted into digital signals and analyzed. 
• Photonics: Lasers use light and atoms to help power quantum technology, while other devices can modify properties of 
photons, such as their path and polarization. 
• Foundries: Quantum development also relies on several types of fabrication facilities, including research fabs at national 
research organizations and universities, pre-industry research fabs, independent foundries that manufacture 
semiconductors for third parties, and in-house fabs operated by quantum technology vendors. 
• Raw materials: Quantum systems also depend on materials used in qubits themselves and in supporting components such 
as cables and cryostats. Helium-3 and helium-4 are used in cryostats to maintain very low temperatures, while rare earth 
metals used in quantum technology include yttrium, neodymium, europium, erbium and ytterbium.15 
AI singularity 
AI singularity refers to a hypothetical point when AI evolves to become self-improving and autonomous, surpassing human 
cognitive abilities.16  
A complementary relationship between quantum and AI 
Rather than developing in isolation, AI and quantum computing could ultimately help advance each other. Quantum computing 
may eventually provide some of the compute power needed to train generative AI, while generative AI could help speed up the 
development and testing of quantum systems.

15 Swayne, M. (2025, October 15). Rare-Earth Minerals And Quantum Computing: How Secure Are The Supply Chains? Quantum Insider. 
16 ISB. (2025, March). What is AI Singularity? Explore Its Future Impact.

### Page 7

02 November 2026    7

I NSTI TUTE

That relationship could become increasingly important as the computing power needed to train the largest generative AI models 
continues to grow exponentially. Classical computing may eventually struggle to keep pace, while quantum computing could help 
meet some of that demand because its computing power also scales exponentially. 
By 2045? 2029? Now? 
AI is becoming increasingly powerful and capable, raising a big question: when could it reach or even surpass human intelligence? 
Futurologist Ray Kurzweil expects AI to reach human levels of intelligence by 2029 and technological singularity by 2045. His 
view extends Moore’s Law beyond processing power to other forms of technological progress. 
Under that framework, computing power would continue growing exponentially. By 2045, Kurzweil projects that $1,000 worth of 
computing could perform as many calculations in one second as all human brains combined.

Marion Laboure | Camilla Siazon |  STABLECOINS: What next for crypto assets?  |  October  20, 2026
STABLECOINS: What next for crypto assets?
Institutional adoption of stablecoins continues to incrementally progress in 2026. While
the frenzy from GENIUS has diminished, stablecoins’ links to the financial system have
continued to deepen, and more policymakers are seriously considering their use cases.
The market cap has hovered around ~$300bn over the past 12 months, a sign that the

stablecoin market is maturing. Stablecoin payments, while still marginal relative to
stablecoins' crypto-native purpose, are also growing, rising from around $103bn in
adjusted transaction volume in 2022 to $375bn in 2026 YTD.
For now, institutional adoption remains concentrated in cross-border payments, treasury
management, 24/7 liquidity provision, collateral, and real-time settlement.
The next big question is whether stablecoins will evolve to become merely a payments

technology, or a genuine disruptor to the global banking system. As we previously
established, the US promotion of stablecoins remains a geopolitical tool to ensure USD
dominance. Bloomberg also reported that the Trump administration is considering joint
ventures with private actors to promote USD stablecoins overseas, in a bid to boost the
dollar’s dominance and source demand for US Treasuries. Last week, Circle announced
plans to expand into 40 new markets in Southeast Asia, LatAm and the Middle East. 99%

of stablecoins (by market cap) remain pegged to USD and are largely backed by US
Treasuries.
Looking ahead, the key debate to watch is whether US regulations will allow for yield-
bearing stablecoins. Research shows that consumers say policymakers should prohibit
crypto companies from offering interest-like rewards for holding stablecoins, which will
threaten to siphon off the bank deposits that drive local lending. The US CLARITY Act
failed in Sep after lawmakers failed to agree on whether the broader crypto market

structure would support such yield-bearing stablecoins.
In this chartbook, we revisit the stablecoin market a year after the GENIUS Act was
signed. We look at: (i) how adoption has evolved, (ii) the emergence of stablecoins as
financial market plumbing via liquidity provisioning and collateral management, and (iii)
the key regulatory debates that will determine the industry’s next direction.
Payment volume has a +66% CAGR

REFRESHER: What are stablecoins?
– Definition: Digital tokens pegged to “stable” assets (e.g. USD or gold), issued
and transferred on a blockchain, which records ownership and transactions.
– Fiat-backed stablecoins are considered fully “collateralized”: USD
stablecoin with 1mn coins in circulation would be backed by $1mn in
cash and equivalents held in reserve by the stablecoin issuer.

– Types: Fiat-, commodity-, crypto- and algorithmic-backed.
– ~99% of stablecoins market valuation is pegged to the USD.
– Market cap: $5bn (2020) to ~$310bn (2026)
– Concentrated in USDT (~60%) and USDC (~23%).
– Benefits: 24/7 transfers & settlement, lower financial intermediaries,
programmability (e.g. escrow, conditional release, automated reconciliation),
and dollar-based store of value.
– Use cases:*
– Crypto trading (~77%) remains dominant.

– Payments (5-12%) growing, cross-border transfers and remittances.
– Store of value (17-33%) especially in emerging markets.
– Other: (~2%-8%): on/off ramps and tokenized RWA settlement.
– Alternatives: CBDCs, tokenized deposits, traditional financial architecture
*Estimates on market share taken from BCG analysis.
Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Haver Analytics, Bloomberg Finance LP. Shows stablecoin total
market cap. Updated October 192026.

ADOPTION OVERVIEW: Most stablecoin volume is still not driven by real economic
activity
Stablecoin transaction volume:
– ~$100trn (unadjusted), ~$2.42trn (adjusted) in 2026 YTD, according to
Allium.
– Chainalysis places more bullish estimates of $28trn in 2025; projected to
reach $719trn by 2035 on organic growth alone.
Stablecoin payments:
– Stablecoin payment volume grew from $151bn in 2022 to $375bn 2026 YTD

(according to VisaOnChain Analytics).
– Businesses account for the majority (58%-64%) of payments, led by B2B
payment volumes.
– Allium Sep report showed largest business payment categories include
service fees ($56bn), payroll ($43bn) and service payments ($32bn).
– Case study: Payments volume across Visa’s >160 stablecoin-linked card
programs across consumer, business and commercial activity grew ~200%
y/y.
Strongest adoption in remittances, B2B payments and dollar access in countries

with unstable currencies or weak banking systems.
– Benefits for cross-border use cases: Instant settlement, <$0.10 per
transaction, borderless, transparent, 24/7, no intermediaries. Remittance fees
are lower (0.5-3.0%) than the global fee average (~6.6%).
– Stablecoins represented 0.31% of fiat cross-border payment value in 2025.
Marginal portion but registered +64% growth increase vs fiat (+9%) according
to Allium. B2B payments had the highest cross-border share (43%).

– A 2025 IMF study also shows transaction flows reached  ~7.7% of GDP in
Latin America and ~6.7% in Africa and the Middle East.

stablecoin payments.

CONSUMER/RETAIL PAYMENT: Still on the periphery
Consumer payments: Not yet there
– Retail adoption is still the most distant use case, especially amongst developed-market consumer credit card spend. A recent ABA survey found that only
24% of consumers think that stablecoins could provide meaningful benefits.
– Stablecoins still look more like a debit instrument than a credit product. They do not naturally replicate the consumer valueproposition of credit cards (i.e.

rewards, revolving credit, dispute rights, chargebacks, fraud protection and convenience), so there is limited incentive for developed-market consumers to
use them.
– Limited scope for payments also evident in wallets (see chart below). Very little of the stablecoin supply is being held as balances in consumer wallets.
Still, the number of retail payments is incrementally growing, with cross-border, emerging market use cases strongest
– Retail transaction volume: $2.6bn (2020) to $71bn (2024); $66bn YTD in 2026.*

– Strongest use in: (i) economies with hyperinflation; (ii) poor banking access; or (iii) high remittance fees.

over quickly, i.e. financial-services firms, treasuries, and wallets. Note: *Retail sized transactions are defined as transactions of $250 or less, according to Visa OnChain Analytics.

Stablecoins can help treasury management and settlement
– Stablecoins have a strong use case for settling transactions for FX,

treasury operations and collateral management i.e. intra-day repos.
– This is because stablecoins’ 24/7 settlement can address
operational frictions in cross-border transactions.
Poses significant risk to cross-border B2B transactions for banks
– Compared to traditional banks, stablecoins offer substantial relief
from transaction costs. Our equity analysts estimate total customer
processing fee savings to increase from 0 to ~$70bn in 2035, if
consumers switch from traditional banks to stablecoins.

Stablecoins is not a replacement solution to FX settlement risk
– FX settlement requires two currencies to be exchanged
simultaneously. Currently, stablecoins only serve as an intermediary
between the two currency legs, resulting in a “stablecoin sandwich”.
– Overwhelming concentration of USD-stablecoins also limits
stablecoin ability to support widespread payment-versus payment
settlement across currency pairs.
– Stablecoins also face fragmented interoperability/liquidity issues

and regulatory uncertainty across issuers/networks/jurisdictions.
– For more, read our FX report here.
Should we explain how settlement work as it is very
interesting and Rohini has a nice chart on this ?
Probably more relevant than having the stablecoin
market cap here I would say.
A more integrated post-trade process
